27.4s	53	def prepare(df: pd.DataFrame, freq: dict) -> pd.DataFrame:
27.4s	54	    """13 kolom mentah (CSV / payload API) -> semua fitur kandidat; tiap model memakai subsetnya sendiri."""
27.4s	55	    X = df[FEATURES].copy()
27.4s	56	    for col, mapping in TO_INT.items():
27.4s	57	        X[col] = X[col].map(mapping)
27.4s	58	    for col, cats in CATEGORIES.items():
27.4s	59	        X[col] = pd.Categorical(X[col], categories=cats)
27.4s	60	    bad = X.columns[X.isna().any()].tolist()
27.4s	61	    if bad:
27.4s	62	        raise ValueError(f"Nilai kosong/tidak dikenal di kolom: {bad}")
27.4s	63	
27.4s	64	    stations = X["Charging_Stations_Near_Home"] + X["Charging_Stations_Near_Work"]
27.4s	65	    X["Total_Charging_Stations"] = stations
27.4s	66	    X["Income_per_Car"] = X["Annual_Income_USD"] / X["Number_of_Cars_Owned"]
27.4s	67	    X["Concern_x_Subsidy"] = X["Environmental_Concern_Level"] * X["Subsidy_Available"]
27.4s	68	    X["Income_x_Concern"] = X["Annual_Income_USD"] * X["Environmental_Concern_Level"]
27.4s	69	    X["Anxiety_x_Commute"] = (X["Range_Anxiety_Level"] + 1) * X["Daily_Commute_km"]
27.4s	70	    X["Commute_per_Station"] = X["Daily_Commute_km"] / (stations + 1)
27.4s	71	    X["Charging_Access"] = (X["Home_Charging_Possible"] + (X["Charging_Stations_Near_Home"] > 0)
27.4s	72	                            + (X["Charging_Stations_Near_Work"] > 0))
27.4s	73	    X["Income_Clipped"] = (X["Annual_Income_USD"] <= 30_000).astype(int)
27.4s	74	    X["Commute_Clipped"] = (X["Daily_Commute_km"] <= 5).astype(int)
27.4s	75	    for col, counts in freq.items():  # nilai yang tidak pernah muncul di train -> 0
27.4s	76	        X[f"{col}_freq"] = X[col].astype(float).map(counts).fillna(0)
27.4s	77	    for col in NUM_AS_CAT:  # string -> cat_feature CatBoost; nilai baru = kategori baru
27.4s	78	        X[f"{col}_cat"] = X[col].astype(float).astype(str).astype(object)
27.4s	79	    return X
